#upgrade palace
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_ookuestate_events.1
	title = cosmo_ookuestate_events.1.t
	desc = cosmo_ookuestate_events.1.d
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoOokuCounter	
			value = 1
			}
		}
			}	
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_INTRODUCTION_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoOokuCounter
			value = 21
			}
			}
		}	
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1C_eventPicture
	}	
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoOokuCounter
			value = 41
			}
		}
		}	
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1D_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoOokuCounter
			value = 61
			}
		}
		}	
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1E_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
		NOT = {
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoOokuCounter
			value = 81
			}
		}
	}	
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1F_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoOokuCounter
			value = 101
			}
	}	
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1G_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoOokuCounter
			value = 121
			}
	}	
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1H_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoOokuCounter
			value = 141
			}
	}	
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1B_eventPicture
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
	}	


	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_ookuestate_events.1.a	
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_1	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_1_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoOokuUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_2	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_2_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoOokuUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_3	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_3_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoOokuUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_4	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_4_upgrade
		}	
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_5	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_5_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoOokuUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_6	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_6_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoOokuUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_7	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_7_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoOokuUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_8	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_8_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoOokuUpgrade
		value = 1
		}
		}								
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_country_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_1_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_2_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_3_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_4_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_5_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_6_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_7_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_8_upgrade	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_all_upgrade
		}
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_1
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_2
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_3
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_4
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_5
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_6
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_7
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_ooku_menu_8						
		}					
}

#Start event from estate
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_ookuestate_events.10
	title = cosmo_ookuestate_events.10.t
	desc = cosmo_ookuestate_events.10.d
	picture = cosmogfx_OOKU_MENU_6B_1_eventPicture

	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		NOT = {
		has_ruler_flag = visiting_concubines
	}
	has_ruler_flag = cosmo_ooku_launch
		
		}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		set_ruler_flag = visiting_concubines
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_ooku_launch
		remove_estate_privilege= estate_ooku_ruler_visit
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines
			}
			random_list = {
				33 = { #adm
						set_ruler_flag = ruler_palace_adm
				}
				33 = { #dip
						set_ruler_flag = ruler_palace_dip
	
				}
				33 = { #mil
						set_ruler_flag = ruler_palace_mil
	
				}																				
			}
		}		
		}
		}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_ookuestate_events.10.a	
		if = {
			limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital #china
				has_country_flag = cosmo_pirate_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_euroharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_purconcubines_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_eurowives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
			}
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_ookuestate_events.30
			}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital #china
				has_country_flag = cosmo_pirate_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_euroharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_purconcubines_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_eurowives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
			}	
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_ookuestate_events.10100
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_isolation_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_ookuestate_events.20
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_advisors_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_onsenaffairs_events.401
			}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_renovate_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_ookuestate_events.1
			}
		}																										
	}					
}

#grant ooku 1a
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_ookuestate_events.11
	title = cosmo_ookuestate_events.11.t
	desc = cosmo_ookuestate_events.11.d
	picture = cosmogfx_OOKU_MENU_1A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = japan_concubine_1a
		}
		}
		
		1014 = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = japan_concubine_1a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = japan_concubine_1a
		}
		set_country_flag = comso_ooku_in_capital
		1014 = {
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = japan_concubine_1a
				duration =  -1
				desc = japan_concubine_1a_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_ookuestate_events.11.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				capital_scope = {
				tag = OTM
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = kyushuan
			dynasty = "Otomo"
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_ooku_1a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_japanmarriage_events.20
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_ookuestate_events.11.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}						
		}					
}

#grant ooku 1b
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_ookuestate_events.12
	title = cosmo_ookuestate_events.12.t
	desc = cosmo_ookuestate_events.12.d
	picture = cosmogfx_OOKU_MENU_1B_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs

		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT

		4182 = {

#grant ooku 1c
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_ookuestate_events.13
	title = cosmo_ookuestate_events.13.t
	desc = cosmo_ookuestate_events.13.d
	picture = cosmogfx_OOKU_MENU_1C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = japan_concubine_1c
		}
		}
		
		4651 = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = japan_concubine_1c
			}

country_event = {
	id = cosmo_ookuestate_events.21
	title = cosmo_ookuestate_events.21.t
	desc = cosmo_ookuestate_events.21.d
	picture = cosmogfx_OOKU_MENU_2A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = japan_concubine_2a
		}
		}
		
		4180 = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
		}
			
	}

#			days = 30
#		}

			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = japan_concubine_2a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = japan_concubine_2a
		}

#grant ooku 3a
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_ookuestate_events.31
	title = cosmo_ookuestate_events.31.t
	desc = cosmo_ookuestate_events.31.d
	picture = cosmogfx_OOKU_MENU_3A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = japan_concubine_3a
		}
		}
		
		1019 = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = japan_concubine_3a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = japan_concubine_3a
		}
		set_country_flag = comso_ooku_in_capital
		1019 = {
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = japan_concubine_3a
				duration =  -1
				desc = japan_concubine_3a_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_ookuestate_events.31.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				capital_scope = {
				tag = AKM
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = japanese
			dynasty = "Akamatsu"
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_ooku_3a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_japanmarriage_events.20
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}

		option = {

		tooltip = {

			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN

#grant ooku 4b
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_ookuestate_events.42
	title = cosmo_ookuestate_events.42.t
	desc = cosmo_ookuestate_events.42.d
	picture = cosmogfx_OOKU_MENU_4B_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = japan_concubine_4b
		}
		}
		
		4185 = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = japan_concubine_4b
			}
			remove_province_modifier = japan_concubine_4b
		}
		set_country_flag = comso_ooku_in_capital
		4185 = {
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = japan_concubine_4b
				duration =  -1
				desc = japan_concubine_4b_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_ookuestate_events.42.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				capital_scope = {
				tag = ISK
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = japanese
			dynasty = "Isshiki"
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_ooku_4b								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_japanmarriage_events.20
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_ookuestate_events.42.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem

			influence = -10
			duration = 3650

	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no

			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace

				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}

			NOT = {

			}	
			}
		}

			culture = japanese
			dynasty = "Kono"
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_ooku_5b

			duration = 3650

#grant ooku 6a
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_ookuestate_events.61
	title = cosmo_ookuestate_events.61.t
	desc = cosmo_ookuestate_events.61.d
	picture = cosmogfx_OOKU_MENU_6A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = japan_concubine_6a
		}
		}
		
		1023 = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = japan_concubine_6a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = japan_concubine_6a
		}
		set_country_flag = comso_ooku_in_capital
		1023 = {
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = japan_concubine_6a
				duration =  -1
				desc = japan_concubine_6a_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_ookuestate_events.61.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				capital_scope = {
				tag = ASA
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = japanese
			dynasty = "Asakura"
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_ooku_6a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_japanmarriage_events.20
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650

			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN

			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = japan_concubine_7b
				duration =  -1
				desc = japan_concubine_7b_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}

		name = cosmo_ookuestate_events.72.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				capital_scope = {
				tag = ODA
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = togoku
			dynasty = "Oda"
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_ooku_7b

#grant ooku 7c
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_ookuestate_events.73
	title = cosmo_ookuestate_events.73.t
	desc = cosmo_ookuestate_events.73.d
	picture = cosmogfx_OOKU_MENU_7C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = japan_concubine_7c
		}
		}
		
		1835 = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = japan_concubine_7c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = japan_concubine_7c
		}
		set_country_flag = comso_ooku_in_capital
		1835 = {
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}

				name = cosmo_has_consort_present

				hidden = yes
				}

				name = japan_concubine_7c
				duration =  -1
				desc = japan_concubine_7c_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}

		name = cosmo_ookuestate_events.73.a

			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}

				any_subject_country = {
				capital_scope = {
				tag = TKI
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"

			}
		}

#grant ooku 10a
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_ookuestate_events.1001
	title = cosmo_ookuestate_events.1001.t
	desc = cosmo_ookuestate_events.1001.d
	picture = cosmogfx_OOKU_MENU_10A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = japan_concubine_10a
		}
		}
		
		4190 = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = japan_concubine_10a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = japan_concubine_10a
		}
		set_country_flag = comso_ooku_in_capital
		4190 = {
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = japan_concubine_10a
				duration =  -1
				desc = japan_concubine_10a_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_ookuestate_events.1001.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				capital_scope = {
				tag = CBA
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = togoku
			dynasty = "Chiba"
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_ooku_10a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_japanmarriage_events.20
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_ookuestate_events.1001.b
		tooltip = {

			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN

		add_estate_influence_modifier = {

#grant ooku 11b
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_ookuestate_events.1012
	title = cosmo_ookuestate_events.1012.t
	desc = cosmo_ookuestate_events.1012.d
	picture = cosmogfx_OOKU_MENU_11B_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = japan_concubine_11b
		}
		}
		
		1026 = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = japan_concubine_11b
			}
			remove_province_modifier = japan_concubine_11b
		}
		set_country_flag = comso_ooku_in_capital
		1026 = {
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = japan_concubine_11b
				duration =  -1
				desc = japan_concubine_11b_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_ookuestate_events.1012.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				capital_scope = {
				tag = DTE
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = togoku
			dynasty = "Date"
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_ooku_11b								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_japanmarriage_events.20
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_ookuestate_events.1012.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_JAPAN
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {

country_event = {

	desc = cosmo_ookuestate_events.1023.d

	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

		ai = no

			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = japan_concubine_12c
		}

		1832 = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
		}

			}
		}

					any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			has_province_modifier = japan_concubine_6a
		}

country_event = {

		ai = no
	}

	hidden_effect = {
		#immediate for palace estate				
		}
		}

		option = {
		name = cosmo_ookuestate_events.1100.a				
		if = {
			limit = {
		NOT = {
					any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			has_province_modifier = japan_concubine_11a
		}
		}		
		}
			custom_tooltip = concubine_gone_tt 		
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_ookuestate_events.101
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
					any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			has_province_modifier = japan_concubine_11a
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_ookuestate_events.1110
			}
		}
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_ookuestate_events.1100.b
		if = {
			limit = {
		NOT = {
					any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			has_province_modifier = japan_concubine_11b
		}
		}		
		}
			custom_tooltip = concubine_gone_tt 		
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_ookuestate_events.101
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
					any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			has_province_modifier = japan_concubine_11b
		}

			limit = {
		NOT = {
					any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			has_province_modifier = japan_concubine_11c
		}
		}		
		}
			custom_tooltip = concubine_gone_tt 		
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_ookuestate_events.101
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
					any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT

		}

		}
		}

			has_province_modifier = japan_concubine_12c
			}
					save_event_target_as = consortprovince		
		}																
		}
	}
	
		option = {
		name = cosmo_ookuestate_events.1230.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines	
			}
			concubine_visit_effect = yes
		   }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines	
			}
			concubine_visit_effect = yes
		   }
		hidden_effect = {

			change_variable = {
				which = cosmoJapaneseCounter
				value = 1
				}					   
			}		   		   		   		   		   		   		   
		hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoOokuTwelveCCounter
		value = 20
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoOokuTwelveCCounter	
			value = 280
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_ooku_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_ooku_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = ooku_kyushuan_theologian
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoOokuTwelveCCounter	
			value = 281
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoOokuTwelveCCounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}	
	}	
}
